# हिन्दी — Hindi / موطأ مالك — Muwatta of Imam Malik

Edition: `deenhindi-web-20261112` · Narrations 1251–1275 of 1836.

[Edition index](README.md) · [Previous](page-0050.md) · [Next](page-0052.md)

## Narration 1251

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1265)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 29</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">قَالَتْ زَيْنَبُ وَسَمِعْتُ أُمِّي أُمَّ سَلَمَةَ، زَوْجَ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم تَقُولُ جَاءَتِ امْرَأَةٌ إِلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَقَالَتْ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ إِنَّ ابْنَتِي تُوُفِّيَ عَنْهَا زَوْجُهَا وَقَدِ اشْتَكَتْ عَيْنَيْهَا أَفَتَكْحُلُهُمَا فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ لاَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ مَرَّتَيْنِ أَوْ ثَلاَثًا كُلُّ ذَلِكَ يَقُولُ ‏&quot;‏ لاَ ‏&quot;‏ ثُمَّ قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ إِنَّمَا هِيَ أَرْبَعَةُ أَشْهُرٍ وَعَشْرًا وَقَدْ كَانَتْ إِحْدَاكُنَّ فِي الْجَاهِلِيَّةِ تَرْمِي بِالْبَعْرَةِ عَلَى رَأْسِ الْحَوْلِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ حُمَيْدُ بْنُ نَافِعٍ فَقُلْتُ لِزَيْنَبَ وَمَا تَرْمِي بِالْبَعْرَةِ عَلَى رَأْسِ الْحَوْلِ فَقَالَتْ زَيْنَبُ كَانَتِ الْمَرْأَةُ إِذَا تُوُفِّيَ عَنْهَا زَوْجُهَا دَخَلَتْ حِفْشًا وَلَبِسَتْ شَرَّ ثِيَابِهَا وَلَمْ تَمَسَّ طِيبًا وَلاَ شَيْئًا حَتَّى تَمُرَّ بِهَا سَنَةٌ ثُمَّ تُؤْتَى بِدَابَّةٍ حِمَارٍ أَوْ شَاةٍ أَوْ طَيْرٍ فَتَفْتَضُّ بِهِ فَقَلَّمَا تَفْتَضُّ بِشَىْءٍ إِلاَّ مَاتَ ثُمَّ تَخْرُجُ فَتُعْطَى بَعْرَةً فَتَرْمِي بِهَا ثُمَّ تُرَاجِعُ بَعْدُ مَا شَاءَتْ مِنْ طِيبٍ أَوْ غَيْرِهِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1265]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1265" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Zaynab said, &quot;I heard my mother, Umm Salama, the wife of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, say that a woman came to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, and said, &#x27;Messenger of Allah! My daughter&#x27;s husband died, and her eyes are troubling her, can she put kohl on them?&#x27; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;No&#x27; two or three times. Then he said, &#x27;It is only four months and ten days. In the Jahiliyya, none of you threw away the piece of dung until a year had passed.&#x27; &quot; Humayd ibn Nafi said, &quot;I asked Zaynab to explain what &#x27;throwing away the piece of dung at the end of a year&#x27; meant. Zaynab said, &#x27;In the Jahiliyya when a woman&#x27;s husband died, she went into a small tent and dressed in the worst of clothes. She did not touch perfume or anything until a year had passed. Then she was brought an animal - a donkey, a sheep, or a bird, and she would break her idda with it, by rubbing her body against it (taftaddu). Rarely did she break her idda with anything (by rubbing herself against it) but that it died. Then she would come out and would be given a piece of dung. She would throw it away and then return to whatever she wished of perfumes or whatever.&#x27; &quot; Malik explained, &#x27;Taftaddu&#x27; means to wipe her skin with it in the same way as with a healing charm</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1265]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1265" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">ज़ैनब ने कहा: मैं अपनी मां उम्मे सलमा के पास गई जो बीवी थीं (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) की। उन्होंने कहा: एक औरत आई रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के पास और कहने लगी: या रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम), मेरी बेटी का ख़ावंद मर गया और उसकी आंखें दुखती हैं, अगर फ़रमाइये तो सुरमा लगा दूं? आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: नहीं नहीं (दो या तीन बार), बल्कि चार महीने दस दिन तक परहेज़ करना ज़रूरी है। जाहिलियत में एक साल तक परहेज़ करते थे, जब साल ख़त्म होता तो ऊंट की मिंगनी फेंकते थे। हुमैद ने कहा: मैं ने ज़ैनब से पूछा ऊंट की मिंगनी फेंकने का क्या मतलब है? उन्होंने कहा: ज़माना-ए-जाहिलियत में जब औरत का ख़ावंद मर जाता तो एक खंडहर में चले जाते और बुरे से बुरे कपड़े पहन लेते, फिर एक साल तक खुशबू वग़ैरह कुछ न लगाते। बाद साल के एक जानवर लाते, गधा या बकरी या कोई परिंदा, उसको अपने बदन पर मलते, अक्सर वो मर जाता। बाद उसके बाहर निकलते तो एक ऊंट की मिंगनी उसको देते, उसको फेंक कर फिर इख्तियार होता चाहे खुशबू लगाए या और कोई काम करे।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1265]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1265" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">زینب نے کہا میں نے اپنی ماں ام سلمہ کے پاس گئی جو بی بی تھیں آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کی انہوں نے کہا ایک عورت آئی ۴سول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے پاس اور کہنے لگی یا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم میری بیٹی کا خاوند مر گیا اور اس کی آنکھیں دکھتی ہیں اگر فرمائیے تو سرمہ لگا دوں آپ نے فرمایا نہیں نہیں دو بار یا تین بار بلکہ چار مہینے دس دن تک پرہیز کرنا ضروری ہے اور جاہلیت میں ایک سال تک پرہیز کرتے تھے جب سال ختم ہوتا تو اونٹ کی مینگنی پھینکتے تھے۔ حمید نے کہا میں نے زینب سے پوچھا اونٹ کی مینگنی پھینکنے کیا مطلب ہے انہوں نے کہا زمانہ جاہلیت میں جب عورت کا خاوند مرجاتا تو ایک کھنڈر میں چلے جاتے اور برے سے برے کپڑے پہن لیتے پھر ایک سال تک خوشبو وغیرہ کچھ نہ لگاتے بعد سال کے ایک جانور لاتے گدھا یا بکری یا کوئی پرندہ اس کو اپنے بدن پر ملتے اکثر وہ مرجاتا بعد اس کے باہر نکلتے تو ایک اونٹ کی مینگنی اس کو دیتے اس کو پھینک کر پھر اختیار ہوتا چاہے خوشبو لگائے یا اور کوئی کام کرے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1265]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1265" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Zaynab ne kaha: Main apni maa Umme Salama ke paas gayi jo biwi thin (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ki. Unhone kaha: Ek aurat aayi Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke paas aur kehne lagi: Ya Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam), meri beti ka khawand mar gaya aur uski aankhein dukhti hain, agar farmayiye to surma laga doon? Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Nahi nahi (do ya teen baar), balki chaar mahine das din tak parhez karna zaroori hai. Jahiliyat mein ek saal tak parhez karte the, jab saal khatam hota to oont ki mingni phenkte the. Humayd ne kaha: Main ne Zaynab se poocha oont ki mingni phenkne ka kya matlab hai? Unhone kaha: Zamana-e-Jahiliyat mein jab aurat ka khawand mar jata to ek khandhar mein chale jate aur bure se bure kapde pehan lete, phir ek saal tak khushbu waghaira kuch na lagate. Baad saal ke ek janwar late, gadha ya bakri ya koi parinda, usko apne badan par malte, aksar wo mar jata. Baad uske bahar nikalte to ek oont ki mingni usko dete, usko phenk kar phir ikhtiyar hota chahe khushbu lagaye ya aur koi kaam kare.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1265]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1265" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1252

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1266)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 29</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ صَفِيَّةَ بِنْتِ أَبِي عُبَيْدٍ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ، وَحَفْصَةَ، زَوْجَىِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ لاَ يَحِلُّ لاِمْرَأَةٍ تُؤْمِنُ</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1266]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1266" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi from Safiyya bint Abi Ubayd from A&#x27;isha and Hafsa, the wives of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;It is not halal for a woman in mourning for someone who has died, if she trusts in Allah and the Last Day, to abstain from adornment for more than three nights, except for a husband</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1266]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1266" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">यहया ने मुझ से इमाम मालिक से, नाफ़ि से, सफिय्या बिंत अबी उबैद से, उम्मुल मोमिनीन हज़रत आइशा और हज़रत हफ़सा रज़ी अल्लाहु अन्हुमा से रिवायत की के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: किसी ऐसी औरत के लिए जो अल्लाह और आख़िरत के दिन पर ईमान रखती हो, ये हलाल नहीं के वो किसी मय्यत पर तीन दिन से ज़्यादा सोग मनाए, सिवाए अपने शौहर के।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1266]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1266" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے مجھ سے امام مالک سے، نافع سے، صفیہ بنت ابی عبید سے، امہات المومنین حضرت عائشہ اور حضرت حفصہ رضی اللہ عنہما سے روایت کی کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: کسی ایسی عورت کے لیے جو اللہ اور آخرت کے دن پر ایمان رکھتی ہو، یہ حلال نہیں کہ وہ کسی میت پر تین دن سے زیادہ سوگ منائے، سوائے اپنے شوہر کے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1266]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1266" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne mujh se Imam Malik se, Nafi se, Safiyya bint Abi Ubayd se, Ummul Momineen Hazrat A&#x27;isha aur Hazrat Hafsa Razi Allahu Anhuma se riwayat ki ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Kisi aisi aurat ke liye jo Allah aur aakhirat ke din par imaan rakhti ho, ye halal nahi ke wo kisi mayyat par teen din se zyada sog manaye, siwaye apne shauhar ke.</p>

## Narration 1253

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1267)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 29</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ أُمَّ سَلَمَةَ، زَوْجَ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَتْ لاِمْرَأَةٍ حَادٍّ عَلَى زَوْجِهَا اشْتَكَتْ عَيْنَيْهَا فَبَلَغَ ذَلِكَ مِنْهَا اكْتَحِلِي بِكُحْلِ الْجِلاَءِ بِاللَّيْلِ وَامْسَحِيهِ بِالنَّهَارِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1267]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1267" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that Umm Salama, the wife of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said to a woman in mourning for her husband whose eyes were troubling her and the pain had become very strong, &quot;Apply jala kohl at night and wipe it off in the day</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1267]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1267" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">यहया ने मुझ से इमाम मालिक से रिवायत की के उन्हों ने सुना था के उम्मुल मोमिनीन हज़रत उम्म सलमा रज़ी अल्लाहु अन्हा ने, जो नबी करीम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) की ज़ौजा थीं, सोगवार औरत से जिस की आँखों में तकलीफ़ थी और दर्द शदीद हो गया था, कहा: रात को जला सुरमा लगाओ और दिन में उसे साफ़ कर लो।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1267]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1267" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے مجھ سے امام مالک سے روایت کی کہ انہوں نے سنا تھا کہ ام المومنین حضرت ام سلمہ رضی اللہ عنہا نے، جو نبی کریم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کی زوجہ تھیں، سوگوار عورت سے جس کی آنکھوں میں تکلیف تھی اور درد شدید ہو گیا تھا، کہا: رات کو جلا سرمہ لگاؤ اور دن میں اسے صاف کر لو۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1267]</p>

## Narration 1254

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1268)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 29</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1268]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1268" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1268</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1268 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ عَنْ سَالِمِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ، وَسُلَيْمَانَ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، أَنَّهُمَا كَانَا يَقُولاَنِ فِي الْمَرْأَةِ يُتَوَفَّى عَنْهَا زَوْجُهَا إِنَّهَا إِذَا خَشِيَتْ عَلَى بَصَرِهَا مِنْ رَمَدٍ أَوْ شَكْوٍ أَصَابَهَا إِنَّهَا تَكْتَحِلُ وَتَتَدَاوَى بِدَوَاءٍ أَوْ كُحْلٍ وَإِنْ كَانَ فِيهِ طِيبٌ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَإِذَا كَانَتِ الضَّرُورَةُ فَإِنَّ دِينَ اللَّهِ يُسْرٌ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1268" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: attributed saying. Attribution: Salim ibn Abdullah and Sulayman ibn Yasar giving the reported advice. Select the specifically transmitted saying of the two named authorities about a widows eye treatment. The following separate maxim by Malik remains compiler commentary.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that Salim ibn Abdullah and Sulayman ibn Yasar said that if a woman whose husband had died feared that an inflammation of her eyes might affect her sight or that some complaint might befall her, she should put kohl on and seek a remedy with kohl or some other cure even if it had perfume in it. </p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1268]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1268" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1268</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1268 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Malik said, &quot;If there is a necessity, the deen of Allah is ease</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1268" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1268]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1268" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1268</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1268 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">हज़रत उम्मे मोमिनीन आयेशा और हज़रत उम्मे मोमिनीन हफ़सा से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: जो औरत ईमान लाए अल्लाह पर और पिछले दिन पर, उसको दुरुस्त नहीं सोग करना किसी मुर्दे पर तीन रातों से ज़्यादा मगर ख़ावंद पर। हज़रत उम्मे सलमा ने एक औरत से कहा जो सोग में थी अपने ख़ावंद के और उसकी आँख दुखती थी, रात को वो सुरमा लगा ले जिससे आँख रोशन हो और दिन को पोंछ डाले। सलीम बिन अब्दुल्लाह और सुलईमान बिन यासर कहते थे: जिस औरत का ख़ावंद मर जाए और उसको आँख के आशोब या किसी और दुख की तकलीफ़ हो, वो सुरमा लगाए और दवा करे, अगरचे इसमें ख़ुशबू हो। मालिक ने कहा: जब ज़रुरत हो तो अल्लाह जल्ला जलालुहु का दीन आसान है।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1268" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حضرت ام المومنین عائشہ اور حضرت ام المومنین حفصة سے رواتی ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا جو عورت ایمان لائے اللہ پر اور پچھلے دن پر اس کو درست نہیں سوگ کرنا کسی مردے پر تین راتوں سے زیادہ مگر خاوند پر ۔ حضرت ام سلمہ نے ایک عورت سے کہا جو سوگ میں تھی پنی خاوند کے اور اس کی آنکھ دکھتی تھی رات کو وہ سرمہ لگالے جس سے آنگھ روشن ہو اور دن کو پونچھ ڈالے ۔ سالم بن عبداللہ اور سلیمان بن یسار کہتے تھے جس عورت کا خاوند مر جائے اور اس کو آنکھ کے آشوب یا کسی اور دکھ کی تکلیف ہو وہ سرمہ لگائے اور دو کرے اگرچہ اس میں خوشبو ہو ۔ کہا مالک نے جب ضرورت ہو تو اللہ جل جلالہ کا دین آسان ہے ۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1268" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1268</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1268 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Hazrat Umme Momineen Ayesha aur Hazrat Umme Momineen Hafsa se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Jo aurat imaan laye Allah par aur pichle din par, usko durust nahi sog karna kisi murde par teen raton se zyada magar khawand par. Hazrat Umme Salama ne ek aurat se kaha jo sog mein thi apne khawand ke aur uski aankh dukhti thi, raat ko wo surma laga le jisse aankh roshan ho aur din ko ponch dale. Salim bin Abdullah aur Sulaiman bin Yasar kehte the: Jis aurat ka khawand mar jaye aur usko aankh ke aashob ya kisi aur dukh ki takleef ho, wo surma lagaye aur dawa kare, agarche ismein khushbu ho. Malik ne kaha: Jab zaroorat ho to Allah Jalla Jalaluhu ka deen aasaan hai.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1268" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1269]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1269" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1269</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1269 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، أَنَّ صَفِيَّةَ بِنْتَ أَبِي عُبَيْدٍ، اشْتَكَتْ عَيْنَيْهَا وَهِيَ حَادٌّ عَلَى زَوْجِهَا عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ فَلَمْ تَكْتَحِلْ حَتَّى كَادَتْ عَيْنَاهَا تَرْمَصَانِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ تَدَّهِنُ الْمُتَوَفَّى عَنْهَا زَوْجُهَا بِالزَّيْتِ وَالشَّبْرَقِ وَمَا أَشْبَهَ ذَلِكَ إِذَا لَمْ يَكُنْ فِيهِ طِيبٌ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَلاَ تَلْبَسُ الْمَرْأَةُ الْحَادُّ عَلَى زَوْجِهَا شَيْئًا مِنَ الْحَلْىِ خَاتَمًا وَلاَ خَلْخَالاً وَلاَ غَيْرَ ذَلِكَ مِنَ الْحَلْىِ وَلاَ تَلْبَسُ شَيْئًا مِنَ الْعَصْبِ إِلاَّ أَنْ يَكُونَ عَصْبًا غَلِيظًا وَلاَ تَلْبَسُ ثَوْبًا مَصْبُوغًا بِشَىْءٍ مِنَ الصِّبْغِ إِلاَّ بِالسَّوَادِ وَلاَ تَمْتَشِطُ إِلاَّ بِالسِّدْرِ وَمَا أَشْبَهَهُ مِمَّا لاَ يَخْتَمِرُ فِي رَأْسِهَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1269" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>English</h3>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1269</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1269 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafic that Saffiyya bint Abi Ubayd suffered from an eye-complaint while she was in mourning for her husband, Abdullah ibn Umar. She did not apply kohl until her eyes almost had ramas (a dry white secretion in the corners of the eye). Malik said, &quot;A woman whose husband has died should anoint her eyes with olive oil and sesame oil and the like of that since there is no perfume in it.&quot; Malik said, &quot;A woman in mourning for her husband should not put on any jewellery - rings, anklets, or such- like, neither should she dress in any sort of colourful, striped garment unless it is coarse. She should not wear any cloth dyed with anything except black, and she should only dress her hair with things like lotus-tree leaves which do not dye the hair</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1269" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1269]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1269" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1269</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1269 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">सफिया बिंत अबू उबैद अपने ख़ावंद यानी अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर के सोग में थीं, उन्होने सुरमा न लगाया और उनकी आँखें दुखती थीं यहाँ तक के चीपड़ आने लगा। मालिक ने कहा: जो औरत सोग में हो अपने ख़ावंद के, वो ज़ेवर क़िस्म से कुछ न पहने, न अँगूठी, न पाये-ज़ेब, न और ज़ेवर, न यमन का कपड़ा मगर जब मोटा और सख़्त हो, न रंगा हो कपड़ा मगर सियाह, न कंघी करे, न खुली डाले मगर बेरी वग़ैरा के पत्तों से बालों को धो सकती है या और किसी चीज़ से जिसमें ख़ुशबू न हो। मालिक से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) उम्मे सलमा (रज़ी अल्लाहु अन्हा) के पास गए और वो अपने ख़ावंद अबू सलमा के सोग में थीं, उन्होने अपनी आँखों पर एलवा लगाया था। आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने पूछा: ए उम्मे सलमा! यह क्या लगाया? उन्होने कहा: या रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम), यह एलवा है। आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: रात को लगाया कर और दिन को पोंछ डाला कर। मालिक ने कहा: अगर औरत नाबालिग हो, उसको हैज़ न आता हो, वो बालिग़ा की तरह है, जब उसका ख़ावंद मर जाए तो सोग करे और जिन उमूर से बालिग़ा को परहेज़ करना लाज़िम है, उन से भी परहेज़ करे। मालिक ने कहा: जब लौंडी का ख़ावंद मर जाए, वो दो महीने पाँच दिन तक सोग करे। इमाम मालिक ने कहा: जब उम्म-ए-वलद का मौला मर जाए तो वो सोग न करे, क्योंकि सोग उन औरतों पर लाज़िम है जो ख़ावंद वालियाँ हों। हज़रत बीबी उम्मे सलमा फ़रमाती थीं: जो औरत सोग में हो, वो अपने सर को बेरी के पत्ते से धो सकती है और ज़ैतून का तेल डाल सकती है।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1269" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="rtl">مالک سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم ام سلمہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہا کے پاس گئے اور وہ اپنے خاوند ابوسلمہ کے سوگ میں تھیں انہوں نے اپنی آنکھوں پر ایلوا لگایا تھا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے نے پوچھا اے ام سلمہ؟ یہ کیا لگایا انہوں نے کہا یا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم یہ ایلوا ہے آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا رات کو لگایا کر اور دن کو پونچھ ڈالا کر ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1269]</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1269</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1269 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">صفیہ بنت ابوعبید اپنے خاوند یعنی عبداللہ بن عمر کے سوگ میں تھیں انہوں نے سرمہ نہ لگایا اور ان کی آنکھیں دکھتی تھیں یہاں تک کہ چیپڑ آنے لگا۔ کہا مالک نے جو عورت سوگ میں ہو اپنے خاوند کے اور وہ زیور قسم سے کچھ نہ پہنے نہ انگوٹھی نہ پائے زیب نہ اور زیوہ نہ یمن کا کپڑا مگر جب موٹا اور سخت ہو نہ رنگا ہو کپڑا مگر سیاہ نہ کنگھی کرے نہ کھلی ڈالے مگر بیری وغیرہ کے پتوں سے بالوں کو دھو سکت ہے یا اور کسی چیز سے جس میں خوشبو نہ ہو </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1269" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="rtl"> کہا مالک نے اگر عورت نابالغ ہو اس کو حیض نہ آتا ہو وہ بالغہ کی طرح ہے جب اس کا خاوند مر جائے تو سوگ کرے اور جن امور سے بالغہ کو پرہیز کرنا لازم ہے ان سے بھی پرہیز کرے ۔ کہا مالک نے جب لونڈی کا خاوند مر جائے وہ دو مہینے پانچ دن تک سوگ کرے۔ کہا امام مالک نے جب ام ولد کا مولیٰ مر جائے تو وہ سوگ نہ کرے کیونکہ سوگ ان عورتوں پر لازم ہے جو خاوند والیاں ہوں ۔ حضرت بی بی ام سلمہ فرماتی تھیں جو عورت سوگ میں ہو وہ اپنے سر کو بیری کے پتے سے دھو سکتی ہے اور زیتون کا تیل ڈال سکتی ہے ۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1269" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1269" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1269 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Safiya bint Abu Ubaid apne khawand yani Abdullah bin Umar ke sog mein thin, unhone surma na lagaya aur unki aankhein dukhti thin yahan tak ke cheepad aane laga. Malik ne kaha: Jo aurat sog mein ho apne khawand ke, wo zevar qisam se kuch na pehne, na angoothi, na paye-zeb, na aur zevar, na Yemen ka kapda magar jab mota aur sakht ho, na ranga ho kapda magar siyah, na kanghi kare, na khuli dale magar beri waghaira ke patton se baalon ko dho sakti hai ya aur kisi cheez se jisme khushbu na ho. Malik se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) Umme Salama (Radi Allahu Anha) ke paas gaye aur wo apne khawand Abu Salama ke sog mein thin, unhone apni aankhon par elwa lagaya tha. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne poocha: Aye Umme Salama! Yeh kya lagaya? Unhone kaha: Ya Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam), yeh elwa hai. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Raat ko lagaya kar aur din ko ponch dala kar. Malik ne kaha: Agar aurat nabaligh ho, usko haiz na aata ho, wo baligha ki tarah hai, jab uska khawand mar jaye to sog kare aur jin umoor se baligha ko parhez karna lazim hai, un se bhi parhez kare. Malik ne kaha: Jab laundi ka khawand mar jaye, wo do mahine paanch din tak sog kare. Imam Malik ne kaha: Jab Umm-e-Walad ka Maula mar jaye to wo sog na kare, kyunki sog un aurton par lazim hai jo khawand waliyan hon. Hazrat Bibi Umme Salama farmati thin: Jo aurat sog mein ho, wo apne sar ko beri ke patte se dho sakti hai aur zaitoon ka tel daal sakti hai.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1269" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1256

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1270)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 29</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم دَخَلَ عَلَى أُمِّ سَلَمَةَ وَهِيَ حَادٌّ عَلَى أَبِي سَلَمَةَ وَقَدْ جَعَلَتْ عَلَى عَيْنَيْهَا صَبِرًا فَقَالَ ‏&quot;‏ مَا هَذَا يَا أُمَّ سَلَمَةَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَقَالَتْ إِنَّمَا هُوَ صَبِرٌ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ اجْعَلِيهِ فِي اللَّيْلِ وَامْسَحِيهِ بِالنَّهَارِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1270]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1270" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, visited Umm Salama while she was in mourning for Abu Salama and she had put aloes on her eyes. He said, &quot;What is this, Umm Salama?&quot; She said, &quot;It is only aloes, Messenger of Allah.&quot; He said, &quot;Put it on at night and wipe it off in the daytime.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The mourning of a young girl who has not yet had a menstrual period takes the same form as the mourning of one who has had a period. She avoids what a mature woman avoids if her husband dies.&quot; Malik said, &quot;A slave-girl mourns her husband when he dies for two months and five nights like her idda.&#x27;&#x27; Malik said, &quot;An umm walad does not have to mourn when her master dies, and a slave-girl does not have to mourn when her master dies. Mourning is for those with husbands</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1270]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1270" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">यहया ने मुझ से इमाम मालिक से रिवायत की के उन्हों ने सुना था के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) हज़रत उम्म सलमा रज़ी अल्लाहु अन्हा के पास तशरीफ़ लाए जब वो अबू सलमा के इंतेक़ाल पर सोगवार थीं और उन्हों ने अपनी आँखों पर एलोवेरा (सबर) लगा रखा था। आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: ये क्या है उम्म सलमा? उन्हों ने कहा: ये सिर्फ़ सबर (एलोवेरा) है, ए अल्लाह के रसूल। आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: इसे रात को लगा लो और दिन में साफ़ कर लो। इमाम मालिक ने कहा: ऐसी छोटी बच्ची का सोग जिस को अभी हैज़ नहीं आया, इसी तरह है जैसे हैज़ वाली औरत का सोग। वो उन चीज़ों से परहेज़ करे गी जिन से शौहर के इंतेक़ाल पर एक बालिग़ औरत परहेज़ करती है। इमाम मालिक ने कहा: बाँदी जब उस का शौहर मर जाए तो दो माह और पाँच रातें सोग मनाए जैसे उस की इद्दत है। इमाम मालिक ने कहा: उम्म-ए-वलद पर अपने आक़ा के इंतेक़ाल पर सोग नहीं, और बाँदी पर भी अपने आक़ा के इंतेक़ाल पर सोग नहीं है। सोग सिर्फ़ उन के लिए है जिन के शौहर हों।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1270]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1270" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے مجھ سے امام مالک سے روایت کی کہ انہوں نے سنا تھا کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم حضرت ام سلمہ رضی اللہ عنہا کے پاس تشریف لائے جب وہ ابو سلمہ کے انتقال پر سوگوار تھیں اور انہوں نے اپنی آنکھوں پر ایلوویرا (صبر) لگا رکھا تھا۔ آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: یہ کیا ہے ام سلمہ؟ انہوں نے کہا: یہ صرف صبر (ایلوویرا) ہے، اے اللہ کے رسول۔ آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: اسے رات کو لگا لو اور دن میں صاف کر لو۔ امام مالک نے کہا: ایسی چھوٹی بچی کا سوگ جس کو ابھی حیض نہیں آیا، اسی طرح ہے جیسے حیض والی عورت کا سوگ۔ وہ ان چیزوں سے پرہیز کرے گی جن سے شوہر کے انتقال پر ایک بالغ عورت پرہیز کرتی ہے۔ امام مالک نے کہا: باندی جب اس کا شوہر مر جائے تو دو ماہ اور پانچ راتیں سوگ منائے جیسے اس کی عدت ہے۔ امام مالک نے کہا: ام ولد پر اپنے آقا کے انتقال پر سوگ نہیں، اور باندی پر بھی اپنے آقا کے انتقال پر سوگ نہیں ہے۔ سوگ صرف ان کے لیے ہے جن کے شوہر ہوں۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1270]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1270" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne mujh se Imam Malik se riwayat ki ke unhon ne suna tha ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) Hazrat Umm Salama Razi Allahu Anha ke paas tashreef laye jab wo Abu Salama ke inteqal par sogwar thin aur unhon ne apni aankhon par aloe vera (sabr) laga rakha tha. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Ye kya hai Umm Salama? Unhon ne kaha: Ye sirf sabr (aloe vera) hai, ae Allah ke Rasool. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Ise raat ko laga lo aur din mein saaf kar lo. Imam Malik ne kaha: Aisi choti bachi ka sog jis ko abhi haiz nahi aaya, isi tarah hai jaise haiz wali aurat ka sog. Wo un cheezon se parhez kare gi jin se shauhar ke inteqal par ek baligh aurat parhez karti hai. Imam Malik ne kaha: Baandi jab us ka shauhar mar jaye to do maah aur paanch raatein sog manaye jaise us ki iddat hai. Imam Malik ne kaha: Umm-e-Walad par apne aaqa ke inteqal par sog nahi, aur baandi par bhi apne aaqa ke inteqal par sog nahi hai. Sog sirf un ke liye hai jin ke shauhar hon.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1270]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1270" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1257

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1271)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 29</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ أُمَّ سَلَمَةَ، زَوْجَ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم كَانَتْ تَقُولُ تَجْمَعُ الْحَادُّ رَأْسَهَا بِالسِّدْرِ وَالزَّيْتِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1271]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that Umm Salama, the wife of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;A mourning woman can rub her head with lotus leaves and olive oil</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1271]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1271" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">यह्या ने मुझे मालिक से बयान किया के उन्होंने सुना के उम्म सलमा, नबी करीम सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम की ज़ौजा मुतहहरा, फरमाती हैं: &#x27;सोगवार औरत बैर के पत्तों और ज़ैतून के तेल से अपने सर पर मालिश कर सकती है।&#x27;</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1271]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1271" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے مجھے مالک سے بیان کیا کہ انہوں نے سنا کہ ام سلمہ، نبی کریم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کی زوجہ مطہرہ، فرماتی ہیں: &#x27;سوگوار عورت بیر کے پتوں اور زیتون کے تیل سے اپنے سر پر مالش کر سکتی ہے۔&#x27;</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1271]</p>

## Narration 1258

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1272)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 30</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي بَكْرٍ، عَنْ عَمْرَةَ بِنْتِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، أَنَّ عَائِشَةَ أُمَّ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ، أَخْبَرَتْهَا أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم كَانَ عِنْدَهَا وَأَنَّهَا سَمِعَتْ صَوْتَ رَجُلٍ يَسْتَأْذِنُ فِي بَيْتِ حَفْصَةَ قَالَتْ عَائِشَةُ فَقُلْتُ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ هَذَا رَجُلٌ يَسْتَأْذِنُ فِي بَيْتِكَ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ أُرَاهُ فُلاَنًا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ لِعَمٍّ لِحَفْصَةَ مِنَ الرَّضَاعَةِ ‏.‏ فَقَالَتْ عَائِشَةُ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ لَوْ كَانَ فُلاَنٌ حَيًّا - لِعَمِّهَا مِنَ الرَّضَاعَةِ - دَخَلَ عَلَىَّ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ نَعَمْ إِنَّ الرَّضَاعَةَ تُحَرِّمُ مَا تُحَرِّمُ الْوِلاَدَةُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1272]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1272" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abdullah ibn Abi Bakr from Amra bint Abd ar-Rahman that A&#x27;isha, umm al-muminin informed her that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, was with her and she heard the voice of a man asking permission to enter the room of Hafsa. A&#x27;isha said that she had said, &quot;Messenger of Allah! There is a man asking permission to enter your house!&quot; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;I think it is so-and-so&quot; (referring to a paternal uncle of Hafsa by suckling). A&#x27;isha said, &quot;Messenger of Allah! If so-and-so were alive (referring to her paternal uncle by suckling) could he enter where I am?&quot; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Yes. Suckling makes haram as birth makes haram</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1272]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1272" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) हज़रत आयशा के घर में उनके पास थे, इतने में हज़रत आयशा ने एक मर्द की आवाज़ सुनी जो हज़रत हफ़सा के घर जाने की इजाज़त चाहता था। हज़रत आयशा बोलिन, या रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) यह कौन शख़्स है जो आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के घर में जाना चाहता है? आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया, मैं समझता हूँ कि फ़लां शख़्स है (हज़रत हफ़सा के रज़ाई चाचा का नाम लिया)। जब हज़रत आयशा ने कहा, या रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम), अगर मेरा रज़ाई चाचा ज़िंदा होता तो क्या मेरे सामने आता? आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया, हां, रज़ाअत हराम करती है जैसे नसब हराम करता है।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1272]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1272" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم حضرت عائشہ کے گھر میں ان کے پاس تھے اتنے میں حضرت عائشہ نے ایک مرد کی آواز سنی جو حضرت حفصہ کے گھر جانے کی اجازت چاہتا تھا حضرت عائشہ بولیں یا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم یہ کون شخص ہے جو آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے گھر میں جانا چاہتا ہے آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا میں سمجھتا ہوں کہ فلاں شخص ہے حضرت حفصہ کے رضاعی چچا کا نام لیا جب حضرت عائشہ نے کہا یا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم اگر میرا رضاعی چچا زندہ ہوتا تو کیا میرے سامنے آتا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا ہاں رضاعت حرام کرتی ہے جیسے نسب حرام کرتا ہے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1272]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1272" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) Hazrat Ayesha ke ghar mein unke paas the, itne mein Hazrat Ayesha ne ek mard ki aawaz suni jo Hazrat Hafsa ke ghar jaane ki ijazat chahta tha. Hazrat Ayesha bolin, Ya Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) yeh kaun shakhs hai jo aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke ghar mein jaana chahta hai? Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya, main samajhta hoon ki falaan shakhs hai (Hazrat Hafsa ke raza&#x27;i chacha ka naam liya). Jab Hazrat Ayesha ne kaha, Ya Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam), agar mera raza&#x27;i chacha zinda hota to kya mere saamne aata? Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya, Haan, raza&#x27;at haram karti hai jaise nasab haram karta hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1272]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1272" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1259

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1273)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 30</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ هِشَامِ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ أُمِّ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ، أَنَّهَا قَالَتْ جَاءَ عَمِّي مِنَ الرَّضَاعَةِ يَسْتَأْذِنُ عَلَىَّ فَأَبَيْتُ أَنْ آذَنَ لَهُ عَلَىَّ حَتَّى أَسْأَلَ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم عَنْ ذَلِكَ فَجَاءَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَسَأَلْتُهُ عَنْ ذَلِكَ فَقَالَ ‏&quot;‏ إِنَّهُ عَمُّكِ فَأْذَنِي لَهُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَتْ فَقُلْتُ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ إِنَّمَا أَرْضَعَتْنِي الْمَرْأَةُ وَلَمْ يُرْضِعْنِي الرَّجُلُ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ ‏&quot;‏ إِنَّهُ عَمُّكِ فَلْيَلِجْ عَلَيْكِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَتْ عَائِشَةُ وَذَلِكَ بَعْدَ مَا ضُرِبَ عَلَيْنَا الْحِجَابُ ‏.‏ وَقَالَتْ عَائِشَةُ يَحْرُمُ مِنَ الرَّضَاعَةِ مَا يَحْرُمُ مِنَ الْوِلاَدَةِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1273]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1273" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Hisham ibn Urwa from his father that A&#x27;isha, umm al-muminin said, &quot;My paternal uncle by suckling came to me and I refused to give him permission to enter until I had asked the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, about it. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, came and I asked him about it. He said, &#x27;He is your paternal uncle, so give him permission.&#x27; So I said, &#x27;Messenger of Allah! The woman nursed me not the man.&#x27; He said, &#x27;He is your paternal uncle, so let him enter.&#x27; &quot; A&#x27;isha said, &quot;That was after the veil had been imposed on us.&quot; A&#x27;isha added, &quot;What is haram by birth is made haram by suckling</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1273]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1273" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हज़रत आयशा ने कहा, मेरा रज़ाई चाचा मेरे पास आया और मुझसे अंदर आने की इजाज़त मांगी। मैंने कहा, रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के पूछे बग़ैर इजाज़त न दूंगी। जब रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) आए तो पूरा वाकिया बताया, आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया, वह तेरा चाचा है तो उसको आने की इजाज़त दे दे। मैंने कहा, या रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम), मुझको तो औरत ने दूध पिलाया था, मर्द का इससे क्या ताल्लुक? आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया, वह तेरा चाचा है बेशक तेरे पास आएगा। और यह गुफ़्तगू उस वक़्त की है जब आयत-ए-हिजाब उतर चुकी थी। हज़रत आयशा ने कहा, जो रिश्ते नसब से हराम हैं वह रज़ाअत से भी हराम हैं।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1273]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1273" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حضرت عائشہ نے کہا میرا رضاعی چچا میرے پاس آیا اور مجھ سے اندر آنے کی اجازت مانگی میں نے کہا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے پوچھے بغیر اجازت نہ دوں گی جب رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم آئے تو پوچھا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا وہ تیرا چچا ہے تو اس کو آنے کی اجازت دے دے میں نے کہا یا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم مجھ کو تو عورت نے دودھ پلایا تھا مرد کا اس سے کیا تعلق آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا وہ تیرا چچا ہے بے شک تیرے پاس آئے گا اور یہ گفتگو اس وقت کی ہے جب آیت حجاب اتر چکی تھی حضرت عائشہ نے کہا جو رشتے نسب سے حرام ہیں وہ رضاعت سے بھی حرام ہیں ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1273]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1273" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Hazrat Ayesha ne kaha, mera raza&#x27;i chacha mere paas aaya aur mujhse andar aane ki ijazat mangi. Maine kaha, Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke pooche baghair ijazat na doongi. Jab Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) aaye to poora waqia bataya, aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya, woh tera chacha hai to usko aane ki ijazat de de. Maine kaha, Ya Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam), mujhko to aurat ne doodh pilaya tha, mard ka isse kya talluq? Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya, woh tera chacha hai beshak tere paas aayega. Aur yeh guftagu us waqt ki hai jab aayat-e-hijab utar chuki thi. Hazrat Ayesha ne kaha, jo rishte nasab se haram hain woh raza&#x27;at se bhi haram hain.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1273]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1273" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Arabic</h3>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ عُرْوَةَ بْنِ الزُّبَيْرِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ أُمِّ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ، أَنَّهَا أَخْبَرَتْهُ أَنَّ أَفْلَحَ أَخَا أَبِي الْقُعَيْسِ جَاءَ يَسْتَأْذِنُ عَلَيْهَا وَهُوَ عَمُّهَا مِنَ الرَّضَاعَةِ بَعْدَ أَنْ أُنْزِلَ الْحِجَابُ قَالَتْ فَأَبَيْتُ أَنْ آذَنَ لَهُ عَلَىَّ فَلَمَّا جَاءَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَخْبَرْتُهُ بِالَّذِي صَنَعْتُ فَأَمَرَنِي أَنْ آذَنَ لَهُ عَلَىَّ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1274" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: The Messenger of Allah answering Aisha about Aflahs requested visit. The full field supplies the milk-kinship relation, refusal, inquiry and instruction to allow the visit.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Urwa ibn az- Zubayr that A&#x27;isha umm al-muminin told him that Aflah, the brother of Abu&#x27;l-Quays came and asked permission to visit her after the veil had been lowered, and he was her paternal uncle by suckling. She said, &quot;I refusedto give him permission to enter. When the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, came, I told him about what I had done, and he ordered me to give him permission to enter</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1274]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1274" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Hazrat Ayesha ne kaha ki mera raza&#x27;i chacha Aflah mere paas aaya aayat-e-hijab ke utarne ke baad, maine usko andar aane ki ijazat na di. Jab Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) aaye to main aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ko is waqia ki khabar di to aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne mere paas use aane ki ijazat de di.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ ثَوْرِ بْنِ زَيْدٍ الدِّيلِيِّ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَبَّاسٍ، أَنَّهُ كَانَ يَقُولُ مَا كَانَ فِي الْحَوْلَيْنِ وَإِنْ كَانَ مَصَّةً وَاحِدَةً فَهُوَ يُحَرِّمُ ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Abbas (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Thawr ibn Zayd ad-Dili that Abdullah ibn Abbas said, &quot;The milk which a child under two years old sucks, even if it is only one suck, makes the foster relatives haram</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1275]</p>

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 30</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ عَمْرِو بْنِ الشَّرِيدِ، أَنَّ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عَبَّاسٍ، سُئِلَ عَنْ رَجُلٍ، كَانَتْ لَهُ امْرَأَتَانِ فَأَرْضَعَتْ إِحْدَاهُمَا غُلاَمًا وَأَرْضَعَتِ الأُخْرَى جَارِيَةً فَقِيلَ لَهُ هَلْ يَتَزَوَّجُ الْغُلاَمُ الْجَارِيَةَ فَقَالَ لاَ اللِّقَاحُ وَاحِدٌ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1276" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Abbas (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Amr ibn ash- Sharid that Abdullah ibn Abbas was asked whether, if a man had two wives, and one of them nursed a slave-boy, and the other had nursed a slave-girl, could the slave-boy marry the slave-girl. He said, &quot;No. The husband is the same</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1276]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1276" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अम्र बिन सुरैद से रिवायत है कि अब्दुल्लाह बिन अब्बास से सवाल हुआ, अगर एक शख़्स की दो बीवियां हों, उन में से एक बीवी एक लड़के को दूध पिलाए और दूसरी बीवी एक लड़की को, क्या उस लड़के का निकाह उस लड़की से दुरुस्त है? जवाब दिया, दुरुस्त नहीं क्योंकि दोनों का बाप एक ही है।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1276" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Amr bin Shureid se riwayat hai ki Abdullah bin Abbas se sawal hua, agar ek shakhs ki do bibiyan hon, un mein se ek bibi ek ladke ko doodh pilaye aur dusri bibi ek ladki ko, kya us ladke ka nikah us ladki se durust hai? Jawab diya, durust nahi kyunki dono ka baap ek hi hai.</p>

<h3>Arabic</h3>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، أَنَّ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ، كَانَ يَقُولُ لاَ رَضَاعَةَ إِلاَّ لِمَنْ أُرْضِعَ فِي الصِّغَرِ وَلاَ رَضَاعَةَ لِكَبِيرٍ ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi that Abdullah ibn Umar said, &quot;There is no kinship by suckling except for a person who is nursed when he is small. There is no kinship by suckling over the age of two years</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1277]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1277" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1264

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1278)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 30</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1278]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1278" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1278</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1278 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، أَنَّ سَالِمَ بْنَ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَخْبَرَهُ أَنَّ عَائِشَةَ أُمَّ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ أَرْسَلَتْ بِهِ وَهُوَ يَرْضَعُ إِلَى أُخْتِهَا أُمِّ كُلْثُومٍ بِنْتِ أَبِي بَكْرٍ الصِّدِّيقِ فَقَالَتْ أَرْضِعِيهِ عَشْرَ رَضَعَاتٍ حَتَّى يَدْخُلَ عَلَىَّ ‏.‏ قَالَ سَالِمٌ فَأَرْضَعَتْنِي أُمُّ كُلْثُومٍ ثَلاَثَ رَضَعَاتٍ ثُمَّ مَرِضَتْ فَلَمْ تُرْضِعْنِي غَيْرَ ثَلاَثِ رَضَعَاتٍ فَلَمْ أَكُنْ أَدْخُلُ عَلَى عَائِشَةَ مِنْ أَجْلِ أَنَّ أُمَّ كُلْثُومٍ لَمْ تُتِمَّ لِي عَشْرَ رَضَعَاتٍ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1278" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: A&#x27;isha (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi that Salim ibn Abdullah ibn Umar informed him that A&#x27;isha umm al-muminin sent him away while he was being nursed to her sister Umm Kulthum bint Abi Bakr as-Siddiq and said, &quot;Suckle him ten times so that he can come in to see me.&quot; Salim said, &quot;Umm Kulthum nursed me three times and then fell ill, so that she only nursed me three times. I could not go in to see A&#x27;isha because Umm Kulthum did not finish for me the ten times</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1278]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1278" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">हज़रत आयशा ने सलीम बिन अब्दुल्लाह को जब वह शीर-ख्वार (दूध पीते) थे, अपनी बहन उम्मे-कुलसूम के पास भेजा, इसलिए कि दस बार उसको दूध पिलाएं ताकि वह बग़ैर परदे के मेरे सामने आ सकें। सलीम ने कहा, उम्मे-कुलसूम ने मुझको तीन बार दूध पिलाया, बाद इसके मैं बीमार हो गया, इसलिए मैं हज़रत आयशा के सामने नहीं जाता क्योंकि मैंने उम्मे-कुलसूम का दस बार दूध नहीं पिया था।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1278" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حضرت عائشہ نے سالم بن عبداللہ کو جب وہ شیر خوار تھے اپنے بہن ام کلثوم کے پاس بھیجا اس لئے کہ دس بار اس کو دودھ پلائی بغیر پردہ کے میرے سامنے آجائیں سالم نے کہا ام کلثوم نے مجھ کو تین بار دودھ پلایا بعد اس کے میں بیمار ہو گیا اس لئے میں حضرت عائشہ کے سامنے نہیں جاتا کیونکہ میں نے ام کلثوم کا دس بار دودھ نہیں پیا تھا ۔</p>

<p dir="ltr">Hazrat Ayesha ne Salim bin Abdullah ko jab woh sheer-khwar (doodh peete) the, apni behn Umm-e-Kulsoom ke paas bheja, isliye ki das baar usko doodh pilayein taaki woh baghair parde ke mere saamne aa sakein. Salim ne kaha, Umm-e-Kulsoom ne mujhko teen baar doodh pilaya, baad iske main bimar ho gaya, isliye main Hazrat Ayesha ke saamne nahi jaata kyunki maine Umm-e-Kulsoom ka das baar doodh nahi piya tha.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1278" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1265

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1279)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 30</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، أَنَّ صَفِيَّةَ بِنْتَ أَبِي عُبَيْدٍ، أَخْبَرَتْهُ أَنَّ حَفْصَةَ أُمَّ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ أَرْسَلَتْ بِعَاصِمِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ سَعْدٍ إِلَى أُخْتِهَا فَاطِمَةَ بِنْتِ عُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ تُرْضِعُهُ عَشْرَ رَضَعَاتٍ لِيَدْخُلَ عَلَيْهَا وَهُوَ صَغِيرٌ يَرْضَعُ فَفَعَلَتْ فَكَانَ يَدْخُلُ عَلَيْهَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1279" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Hafsa (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi that Safiyya bint Abi Ubayd told him that Hafsa, umm al-muminin, sent Asim ibn Abdullah ibn Sad to her sister Fatima bint Umar ibn al-Khattab for her to suckle him ten times so that he could come in to see her. She did it, so he used to come in to see her</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1279]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1279" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">सफ़िया बिन अबी उबैद से रिवायत है कि उम्मुल-मोमिनीन हफ़्सा ने आसिम बिन अब्दुल्लाह बिन साद को, जब वह शीर-ख्वार थे, अपनी बहन फ़ातिमा बिन्त उमर बिन ख़त्ताब के पास भेजा ताकि उनको दस मर्तबा दूध पिलाएं ताकि जब वह बड़े हो जाएं तो उनके सामने हुआ करें। फ़ातिमा ने आसिम को दूध पिला दिया, फिर आसिम जब बड़े हुए तो हज़रत हफ़्सा उनके सामने हुआ करतीं।</p>

<details>

<p dir="ltr">Safiya bin Abi Ubaid se riwayat hai ki Umm-ul-Momineen Hafsa ne Asim bin Abdullah bin Saad ko, jab woh sheer-khwar the, apni behn Fatima bint Umar bin Khattab ke paas bheja taaki unko das martaba doodh pilayein taaki jab woh bade ho jayen to unke saamne hua karein. Fatima ne Asim ko doodh pila diya, phir Asim jab bade hue to Hazrat Hafsa unke saamne hua kartin.</p>

## Narration 1266

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1280)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 30</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ الْقَاسِمِ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّهُ أَخْبَرَهُ أَنَّ عَائِشَةَ زَوْجَ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم كَانَ يَدْخُلُ عَلَيْهَا مَنْ أَرْضَعَتْهُ أَخَوَاتُهَا وَبَنَاتُ أَخِيهَا وَلاَ يَدْخُلُ عَلَيْهَا مَنْ أَرْضَعَهُ نِسَاءُ إِخْوَتِهَا ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1280]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1280" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1280</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1280 ]</p>

</details>

<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abd ar-Rahman ibn al-Qasim that his father told him that A&#x27;isha, the wife of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, admitted those whom her sisters and the daughters of her brother had nursed, and she did not admit those who were nursed by the wives of her brothers</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1280]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1280" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1280</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">क़ासिम बिन मुहम्मद से रिवायत है कि हज़रत आयशा उन लोगों के सामने होती थीं जिन को दूध पिलाया था, उनकी बहनों और भतीजियों के, और उन लोगों के सामने नहीं होती थीं जिन को दूध पिलाया था उनकी भाभियों ने।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1280]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1280" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">قاسم بن محمد سے روایت ہے کہ حضرت عائشہ سامنے ہوتیں ان لوگوں کے جن کو دودھ پلایا تھا ان کی بہنوں اور بھیتجوں کے اور نہیں سامنے ہوتی تھیں ان لوگوں کے جن کو دودھ پلایا تھا ان کی بھاوجوں نے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1280]</p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Qasim bin Muhammad se riwayat hai ki Hazrat Ayesha un logon ke saamne hoti thin jin ko doodh pilaya tha, unki behnon aur bhateejiyon ke, aur un logon ke saamne nahi hoti thin jin ko doodh pilaya tha unki bhabhiyon ne.</p>

## Narration 1267

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1281)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 30</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1281]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1281" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1281</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1281 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ إِبْرَاهِيمَ بْنِ عُقْبَةَ، أَنَّهُ سَأَلَ سَعِيدَ بْنَ الْمُسَيَّبِ عَنِ الرَّضَاعَةِ، فَقَالَ سَعِيدٌ كُلُّ مَا كَانَ فِي الْحَوْلَيْنِ وَإِنْ كَانَتْ قَطْرَةً وَاحِدَةً فَهُوَ يُحَرِّمُ وَمَا كَانَ بَعْدَ الْحَوْلَيْنِ فَإِنَّمَا هُوَ طَعَامٌ يَأْكُلُهُ ‏.‏ قَالَ إِبْرَاهِيمُ بْنُ عُقْبَةَ ثُمَّ سَأَلْتُ عُرْوَةَ بْنَ الزُّبَيْرِ فَقَالَ مِثْلَ مَا قَالَ سَعِيدُ بْنُ الْمُسَيَّبِ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1281" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">This source entry preserves two separately attributed reports in their original order. They remain one provider entry, with their distinct narrator introductions and source references. Report type: successor report. Attribution: Said ibn al-Musayyab (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words. Report type: abbreviated transmission. Attribution: Urwa ibn az-Zubayr (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Ibrahim ibn Uqba asked Said ibn al-Musayyab about suckling. Said said, &quot;All that occurs in the first two years, even if it is only a drop, makes haram. Whatever is after two years, is only food that is eaten.&quot;</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 2</p>

<p dir="ltr">Ibrahim ibn Uqba said, &#x27;&#x27;Then I asked Urwa ibn az-Zubayr and he told me the same as what Said ibn al-Musayyab said</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1281]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1281" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">इब्राहिम बिन उतबा ने सईद बिन मुसय्यब से रिज़ाअत (दूध पिलाने) का हुकुम पूछा। सईद ने कहा जो रिज़ाअत दो बरस के अंदर हो उस से हुरमत साबित हो जाएगी अगरचे एक क़तरा ही क्यों न हो, और जो दो बरस के बाद हो उस से हुरमत साबित न होगी, बल्कि वह खाने पीने की तरह है। इब्राहिम ने कहा फिर मैं ने उरवाह बिन ज़ुबैर से पूछा तो उन्होंने भी ऐसा ही कहा।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1281" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">ابراہیم بن عتبہ نے سعید بن مسیب سے پوچھا رضاعت کا حکم سعید نے کہا جو رضاعت دو برس کے اندر ہو اس سے حرمت ثابت ہو جائے گی اگرچہ ایک قطرہ ہو اور جو دو برس کے بعد اس سے حرمت ثابت نہ ہوگی بلکہ وہ مثل کھانوں کے ہے ابراہم نے کہا پھر میں نے عروہ بن زبیر سے پوچھا انہوں نے بھی ایسا ہی کہا ۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Ibrahim bin Utba ne Saeed bin Musayyib se riza&#x27;at (doodh pilane) ka hukum pucha. Saeed ne kaha jo riza&#x27;at do baras ke andar ho us se hurmat sabit ho jayegi agarche ek qatra hi kyun na ho, aur jo do baras ke baad ho us se hurmat sabit na hogi, balki woh khane peene ki tarah hai. Ibrahim ne kaha phir main ne Urwah bin Zubair se pucha to unhone bhi aisa hi kaha.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1281" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Said ibn al-Musayyab (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Yahya ibn Said said that he heard Said ibn al-Musayyab say, &quot;Suckling is only while the child is in the cradle. If not, it does not cause flesh and blood relations</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1282]</p>

## Narration 1269

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 30</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، أَنَّهُ كَانَ يَقُولُ الرَّضَاعَةُ قَلِيلُهَا وَكَثِيرُهَا تُحَرِّمُ وَالرَّضَاعَةُ مِنْ قِبَلِ الرِّجَالِ تُحَرِّمُ ‏.‏ قَالَ يَحْيَى وَسَمِعْتُ مَالِكًا يَقُولُ الرَّضَاعَةُ قَلِيلُهَا وَكَثِيرُهَا إِذَا كَانَ فِي الْحَوْلَيْنِ تُحَرِّمُ فَأَمَّا مَا كَانَ بَعْدَ الْحَوْلَيْنِ فَإِنَّ قَلِيلَهُ وَكَثِيرَهُ لاَ يُحَرِّمُ شَيْئًا وَإِنَّمَا هُوَ بِمَنْزِلَةِ الطَّعَامِ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1283" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Ibn Shihab (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab that he said, &quot;Suckling however little or much, makes haram. Kinship by suckling makes men mahram.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1283]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1283" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Yahya said that he had heard Malik say, &quot;Suckling, however little or much when it is in the first two years, makes haram. As for what is after the first two years, little or much, it does not make anything haram. It is like food</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">इब्न शहाब कहते थे कि रिज़ाअत थोड़ी हो या ज़्यादा, हुरमत साबित कर देती है और रिज़ाअत मरूरन (यानी बार-बार दूध पिलाने) से भी हुरमत साबित हो जाती है। यहया ने कहा कि इमाम मालिक कहते थे दो बरस के अंदर रिज़ाअत कलील (कम) हो या कसीर (ज़्यादा), हुरमत साबित कर देती है और दो बरस के बाद की रिज़ाअत से हुरमत साबित नहीं होती बल्कि वह आम खानों की तरह है।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1283" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Ibn Shahab kehte the ki riza&#x27;at thodi ho ya zyada, hurmat sabit kar deti hai aur riza&#x27;at marooran (yani bar-bar doodh pilane) se bhi hurmat sabit ho jati hai. Yahya ne kaha ki Imam Malik kehte the do baras ke andar riza&#x27;at qaleel (kam) ho ya kaseer (zyada), hurmat sabit kar deti hai aur do baras ke baad ki riza&#x27;at se hurmat sabit nahi hoti balki woh aam khano ki tarah hai.</p>

## Narration 1270

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1284)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 30</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، أَنَّهُ سُئِلَ عَنْ رَضَاعَةِ الْكَبِيرِ، فَقَالَ أَخْبَرَنِي عُرْوَةُ بْنُ الزُّبَيْرِ، أَنَّ أَبَا حُذَيْفَةَ بْنَ عُتْبَةَ بْنِ رَبِيعَةَ، وَكَانَ، مِنْ أَصْحَابِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَكَانَ قَدْ شَهِدَ بَدْرًا وَكَانَ تَبَنَّى سَالِمًا الَّذِي يُقَالُ لَهُ سَالِمٌ مَوْلَى أَبِي حُذَيْفَةَ كَمَا تَبَنَّى رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم زَيْدَ بْنَ حَارِثَةَ وَأَنْكَحَ أَبُو حُذَيْفَةَ سَالِمًا وَهُوَ يَرَى أَنَّهُ ابْنُهُ أَنْكَحَهُ بِنْتَ أَخِيهِ فَاطِمَةَ بِنْتَ الْوَلِيدِ بْنِ عُتْبَةَ بْنِ رَبِيعَةَ وَهِيَ يَوْمَئِذٍ مِنَ الْمُهَاجِرَاتِ الأُوَلِ وَهِيَ مِنْ أَفْضَلِ أَيَامَى قُرَيْشٍ فَلَمَّا أَنْزَلَ اللَّهُ تَعَالَى فِي كِتَابِهِ فِي زَيْدِ بْنِ حَارِثَةَ مَا أَنْزَلَ فَقَالَ ‏{‏ادْعُوهُمْ لآبَائِهِمْ هُوَ أَقْسَطُ عِنْدَ اللَّهِ فَإِنْ لَمْ تَعْلَمُوا آبَاءَهُمْ فَإِخْوَانُكُمْ فِي الدِّينِ وَمَوَالِيكُمْ‏}‏ رُدَّ كُلُّ وَاحِدٍ مِنْ أُولَئِكَ إِلَى أَبِيهِ فَإِنْ لَمْ يُعْلَمْ أَبُوهُ رُدَّ إِلَى مَوْلاَهُ فَجَاءَتْ سَهْلَةُ بِنْتُ سُهَيْلٍ وَهِيَ امْرَأَةُ أَبِي حُذَيْفَةَ وَهِيَ مِنْ بَنِي عَامِرِ بْنِ لُؤَىٍّ إِلَى رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَقَالَتْ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ كُنَّا نَرَى سَالِمًا وَلَدًا وَكَانَ يَدْخُلُ عَلَىَّ وَأَنَا فُضُلٌ وَلَيْسَ لَنَا إِلاَّ بَيْتٌ وَاحِدٌ فَمَاذَا تَرَى فِي شَأْنِهِ فَقَالَ لَهَا رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ أَرْضِعِيهِ خَمْسَ رَضَعَاتٍ فَيَحْرُمُ بِلَبَنِهَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ وَكَانَتْ تَرَاهُ ابْنًا مِنَ الرَّضَاعَةِ فَأَخَذَتْ بِذَلِكَ عَائِشَةُ أُمُّ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ فِيمَنْ كَانَتْ تُحِبُّ أَنْ يَدْخُلَ عَلَيْهَا مِنَ الرِّجَالِ فَكَانَتْ تَأْمُرُ أُخْتَهَا أُمَّ كُلْثُومٍ بِنْتَ أَبِي بَكْرٍ الصِّدِّيقِ وَبَنَاتِ أَخِيهَا أَنْ يُرْضِعْنَ مَنْ أَحَبَّتْ أَنْ يَدْخُلَ عَلَيْهَا مِنَ الرِّجَالِ وَأَبَى سَائِرُ أَزْوَاجِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنْ يَدْخُلَ عَلَيْهِنَّ بِتِلْكَ الرَّضَاعَةِ أَحَدٌ مِنَ النَّاسِ وَقُلْنَ لاَ وَاللَّهِ مَا نَرَى الَّذِي أَمَرَ بِهِ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم سَهْلَةَ بِنْتَ سُهَيْلٍ إِلاَّ رُخْصَةً مِنْ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فِي رَضَاعَةِ سَالِمٍ وَحْدَهُ لاَ وَاللَّهِ لاَ يَدْخُلُ عَلَيْنَا بِهَذِهِ الرَّضَاعَةِ أَحَدٌ فَعَلَى هَذَا كَانَ أَزْوَاجُ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم فِي رَضَاعَةِ الْكَبِيرِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1284]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1284" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab that he was asked about the suckling of an older person. He said, &#x27;&#x27;Urwa ibn az-Zubayr informed me that Abu Hudhayfa ibn Utba ibn Rabia, one of the companions of the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, who was present at Badr, adopted Salim (who is called Salim, the mawla of Abu Hudhayfa) as the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, adopted Zayd ibn Haritha. He thought of him as his son, and Abu Hudhayfa married him to his brother&#x27;s sister, Fatima bint al-Walid ibn Utba ibn Rabia, who was at that time among the first emigrants. She was one of the best unmarried women of the Quraysh. When Allah the Exalted sent down in His Book what He sent down about Zayd ibn Haritha, &#x27;Call them after their true fathers. That is more equitable in the sight of Allah. If you do not know who their fathers were then they are your brothers in the deen and your mawali,&#x27; (Sura 33 ayat 5) people in this position were traced back to their fathers. When the father was not known, they were traced to their mawla. &quot;Sahla bint Suhayl who was the wife of Abu Hudhayfa, and one of the tribe of Amr ibn Luayy, came to the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, and said, &#x27;Messenger of Allah! We think of Salim as a son and he comes in to see me while I am uncovered. We only have one room, so what do you think about the situation?&#x27; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;Give him five drinks of your milk and he will be mahram by it.&#x27; She then saw him as a foster son. A&#x27;isha umm al-muminin took that as a precedent for whatever men she wanted to be able to come to see her. She ordered her sister, Umm Kulthum bint Abi Bakr as-Siddiq and the daughters of her brother to give milk to whichever men she wanted to be able to come in to see her. The rest of the wives of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, refused to let anyone come in to them by such nursing. They said, &#x27;No! By Allah! We think that what the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, ordered Sahla bint Suhayl to do was only an indulgence concerning the nursing of Salim alone. No! By Allah! No one will come in upon us by such nursing!&#x27; &quot;This is what the wives of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, thought about the suckling of an older person</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1284]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1284" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>
